<span class="ct-node-label">Outcomes</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">Does Better Thinking Change Real Choices?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">The most useful test of thinking instruction is whether people handle new decisions differently, not whether they can define concepts.</span>
</span>

<span class="ct-node-label">Near vs Far</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">How Far Can a Thinking Skill Travel?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Near transfer is easier than far transfer because the new situation shares more visible cues with the original practice.</span>
